Jaafar Jackson Shares What Jackson Family Holiday Festivities Are Like

📅 Nov 17, 2026  |  Category: Entertainment  |  Read Source →

Jaafar Jackson shared how he celebrates the holidays with his fellow family members during an exclusive interview with E! News at the CCA's 9th Annual Celebration of Black Cinema & Television.

Jaafar Jackson will always remember having a good time around the holidays.

In fact, as the Michael star—son to Jermaine Jackson and Alejandra Genevieve Oaziaza—explained, his family finds more than one way to rock with one another during the festivities.

“It’s a lot of great music playing, there’s a lot of good food,” he exclusively told E! News at the CCA's 9th Annual Celebration of Black Cinema & Television Nov. 16. “We usually love playing some board games. Pictionary is one of our favorites. It can be loud at times, I would say, but it’s great, you know, it’s wonderful.”

Also wonderful, as Jaafar admitted, would be what’s on the table, too. “There’s always some peach cobbler, apple pie,” the 30-year-old noted. “And I usually go it first—to the dessert spread.”

Sweet treats and family gatherings aside, Jafaar—who moonwalked across theater screens earlier this year with his portrayal of his uncle Michael Jackson—is also gearing up to star in the film’s sequel. However, if you’re wondering about all the details to come, you are not alone.

“I haven’t started prepping just yet, it’s so early on—I can’t really say anything,” Jafaar shared. “It’s in the early works so we’ll see as time comes. And I don’t want to give anything away but it’s going to be a very special, incredible journey, I will say.”

But for now, Jafaar is enjoying what the man in the mirror has accomplished thus far with his role.

“It’s so much to take in,” he explained of the response. “I’ve been just overwhelmed with just so much joy and gratitude because it’s been a wonderful experience for me to see how much this film has connected with people.”

“It’s brought back that nostalgia feeling to a lot of people,” Jaafar reflected, “who grew up knowing Michael and experiencing his music and artistry. So, for me to be a part of that and play a hand in bringing forth his music to a new generation, I couldn’t be more grateful for that.”

For a deeper dive into everything the Jackson family has said about the Michael movie so far, keep reading…

As a producer on the film, Michael Jackson's eldest child "was on set every day," lead producer Graham King told The Hollywood Reporter at the film's April 20 Los Angeles premiere.

Prince, who attended premieres in L.A. and Berlin, has also touted his cousin Jaafar Jackson's performance, writing April 11 on Instagram that watching Jermaine Jackson's son "step into the role of his uncle Michael is incredibly special for the family. With his talent, dedication and spirit, he was the only right choice to portray Michael and bring his story to the big screen."

When Colman Domingo, who plays her late grandfather Joe Jackson, said that Prince and Paris were "very much in support" of Michael, Paris begged to differ.

“Don’t be telling people I was ‘helpful’ on the set of a movie I had 0% involvement in lol,” Michael's daughter wrote on her Instagram Story in August 2025. “That is so weird.”

Rather, she continued, "I read one of the first drafts of the script and gave my notes about what was dishonest / didn’t sit right with me and when they didn’t address it I moved on with my life. Not my monkeys not my circus. God bless and god speed.”

She crystalized her take the following month, saying in a series of Instagram videos, "They’re gonna make whatever they’re gonna make. The big reason why I haven’t said anything up until this point is because I know a lot of you guys are gonna be happy with it" because it "panders to a very specific section of my dad’s fandom that still lives in a fantasy."

Hollywood biopics are "sold to you as real," Paris said, but they're "sugar-coated."

Meaning, she alleged, "The narrative is being controlled. There’s a lot of inaccuracy and there’s a lot of full-blown lies. At the end of the day, that doesn’t really fly with me. I don’t really like dishonesty."

Paris also pushed back against accusations that she "hates her dad…because I'm not, like, calling the shots on set being a big-shot producer of a movie that's filled with just inaccuracies. That's not my truth…I just prefer honesty over sales and monetary gain."

As the most private of Michael's kids, Bigi hasn't commented on the film, but he attended the April 10 premiere in Berlin.

“To have my cousins there and my other family members, that meant everything,” Jaafar told E! News April 20. “Because it is a family story, and it all started in Gary, Indiana, so it meant the world to me.”

Michael's sister, the youngest of Joe and Katherine Jackson's 10 kids, asked to be removed from this narrative.

“I wish everybody was in the movie,” sister LaToya Jackson told Variety at the L.A. premiere. But Janet "was asked and she kindly declined so you have to respect her wishes.”

In response to a March Page Six report that Janet had issues with almost every scene in the film and drew Jermaine's ire during a family screening, La Toya told Entertainment Tonight, "There was absolutely no problem whatsoever, none whatsoever. Please believe it."

Director Antoine Fuqua said he has "so much respect and love for Janet," telling Variety she was "supportive of Jaafar, and that's what matters."

Jaafar was "absolutely fantastic," La Toya told E! News at the L.A. premiere. "He just embodied my brother and I'm so proud of him."

The fifth-eldest Jackson sibling considered it "very important" that her family collaborated on the movie, telling THR, "A lot of times people think they know the story and they read about things, but when the family’s involved, the family can say yay or nay."

Jaafar left Marlon "smiling inside at how great he is," the sixth-eldest Jackson sibling told E!, adding that he assured his nephew that his performance was "unbelievable."

And Marlon said he didn't spot any inaccuracies in the film, sharing, "When I first saw him on stage, when I went to the set the first time, they were shooting the [Jacksons' 1984] Victory Tour…And I said, 'Wow.' He looked just like Michael."

Jaafar's father, suffice it to say, was "extremely proud," telling E!, "He deserved the part, he worked so hard."

But before Jaafar took on the role, Jermaine explained to Fab TV at the Berlin premiere, he wanted his son to understand "what he was going up against," because those "are a tough set of shoes to fill."

Michael's nephew Taj Jackson told critics to beat it.

"Sorry, media, you don’t get to control the narrative anymore of who Michael Jackson truly was," the eldest son of late Jackson 5 member Tito Jackson wrote on X April 21, per Variety. "The public gets to watch this movie… they will decide for themselves. And you can’t handle that. Can’t wait until some critics have to eat crow. And yes, I will be that petty."

Tito's youngest son wrote on X April 22 that he was headed out to rewatch Michael, because he "wouldn't miss this opportunity to be masked in the electrifying energy that will be in that theater for nothing. My uncle deserves this, my cousin earned this and his loyal supporters are owed this. The world will be reminded or learn who MJ truly was and I can’t wait!"

Roadside Attractions Acquires U.S. Rights to Thriller ‘One Second After’ Based on William R. Forstchen Novel (EXCLUSIVE)

📅 Nov 17, 2026  |  Category: Entertainment  |  Read Source →

Roadside Attractions has acquired U.S. distribution rights to “One Second After,” Scott Rogers’ action thriller based on the 2009 novel by William R. Forstchen. The film is set for theatrical release on April 30, 2027.

“One Second After” marks Roger’s directorial debut. J. Michael Straczynski and Nicole Roewe penned the screenplay. Rob Pfaltzgraff, Nick Reid, Lana Link and Matt Kennedy serve as producers. Executive producers include Richard Masson, Sue Masson, Kenneth Horowitz, Barbara Horowitz, David Gore, Cecilia Gore and Forstchen. “One Second After” is an MPI Original Films production in association with Startling. Related Stories The Game Awards Nominations 2026: 'GTA 6' Lands Game of the Year Nom Ahead of Thursday Release Connor Storrie, Inde Navarrette, Kane Parsons Among 14 Industry Insiders Tapped for 2026 BAFTA Breakthrough U.S. Cohort

According to an official logline, “One Second After” follows “former Army Colonel turned college professor John Matherson as a devastating electromagnetic pulse attack instantly disables the nation’s electrical grid, communications and transportation systems. With modern civilization brought to a standstill, Matherson must rally his neighbors to protect their families and community as dwindling resources, mounting desperation and violent outsiders threaten their survival.”

Josh Holloway stars alongside Hannah John-Kamen, Steven Ogg, Famke Janssen and Mary McDonnell.

“’One Second After’ takes a very real concern about the vulnerability of our infrastructure and brings it to life through the story of one family,” said Howard Cohen and Eric d’Arbleoff, Roadside Attractions co-presidents. “We live in a world where we take so much for granted, and the film asks what happens when those systems suddenly disappear. We love that the film can explore something so relevant while being a gripping, wildly entertaining ride.”

Roadside Attraction’s other upcoming films include “The Gallerist,” a crime comedy starring Natalie Portman, Jenna Ortega, Da’Vine Joy Randolph, Sterling K. Brown and Zach Galifianakis, and “Babies” starring Seth Rogen, Anna Kendrick, Issa Rae and Dan Stevens.

Jena Malone, Suzanna Son & Sofia Mortera Wrap On Indie ‘Kismet’ From Giovanna Molina

📅 Nov 17, 2026  |  Category: Entertainment  |  Read Source →

EXCLUSIVE: Jena Malone (The Boroughs) and Suzanna Son (Monster: The Ed Gein Story) have wrapped roles opposite newcomer Sofia Mortera in Kismet, an indie from Radiance Pictures and Distant Films that marks the feature debut of writer-director Giovanna Molina.

Set during the desolate off-season on Fire Island, Kismet follows a melancholic 13-year-old girl (Mortera) who, in the wake of her parents’ separation, claims an alien has taken over her body to save her from her human emotions.

Recently wrapping production, Kismet co-stars Elliot Villar and Riley Vinson, with Zuzanna Szadkowski, Matthew Sean Blumm, Marta Pozzan, Caitlin Hagney, David Staats and Lorena Ramos rounding out the cast. The film is produced by Molina, along with Zoey Pressey, Alexander Spenser Gould and Nuer Taqa. It’s cast by Betsy Fippinger, CSA. Related Stories News 'Hollow Moon': Sci-Fi With Jena Malone & Dermot Mulroney Gets Sales Deal & New Title News Jena Malone, Dermot Mulroney, Mekhi Phifer & Vannessa Vasquez Set A Mission As 'Moon People'

Molina told Deadline, “It felt incredibly special to bring this talented cast and crew this fall to Fire Island, where I spent much of my childhood. Jena Malone is a coming-of-age sci-fi icon, so it’s truly full circle to have her supporting a young exciting newcomer like Sofia Mortera in a film that nods to DONNIE DARKO through a female lens. Throughout our shoot, the island took on a mythical shape that mirrored the uncanny nature of the film: beached whales, nor’easter floods, roaming deer, and a double rainbow, to name a few. Filming against this surreal backdrop was a remarkable experience.” Watch on Deadline

A Golden Globe nominee, Malone has most recently been seen in the Netflix series The Boroughs, exec produced by The Duffer Brothers, as well as Jack Begert’s indie Junk, produced by Darren Aronofsky. Upcoming, she’ll be seen in the horror thriller Pendulum and Arkasha Stevenson’s untitled horror film for A24. She is represented by Entertainment 360, Gersh and Hirsch Wallerstein Hayum.

Son is best known for her breakout turn opposite Simon Rex in Sean Baker’s Red Rocket, for which she was nominated for the Gotham Award for Breakout Performer as well as the Independent Spirit Award for Best Supporting Female. She recently appeared in Monster: The Ed Gein Story and will also be seen coming up in A24’s mystery film from Stevenson. She is repped by Curate, CAA and Jackoway Austen Tyerman.

Mortera voices Young Priscilla in Netflix’s animated feature Steps and has appeared in the films Come Out and Play and The Spirit of Innocence. Additional voiceover credits include Netflix’s CoComelon Lane and Prime Video’s Agent Zeta. She is repped by DDO Artists Agency and Mills Kaplan Entertainment.

A Mexican-Italian-American filmmaker from Brooklyn, NY, Molina’s work explores stories of isolation, belief and surrealism within a female coming-of-age context. She has received grants and fellowships from Sundance, PBS, The Future of Film is Female and the Saul Zaentz Innovation Fund. Her film Quaker had its world premiere at the 75th Berlin Film Festival and won Best Narrative Short at Bend Film Festival. She holds an MFA in Directing from the UCLA School of Theater, Film and Television and a BA from Johns Hopkins University, with degrees in The Writing Seminars and Film & Media Studies. She is represented by Circle Management + Production.

Inde Navarrette Credits Actresses Who Gave Her ‘Permission to Be Free’

📅 Nov 17, 2026  |  Category: Entertainment  |  Read Source →

For ELLE Women in Hollywood honoree Inde Navarrette, playing Nikki in Obsession was about far more than landing her first leading role. In her acceptance speech at our annual event at the Four Seasons Hotel Los Angeles at Beverly Hills last night, the actress spoke about putting everything that she had into playing the character. “I needed to break through every glass ceiling either school, peers, society, or even I put on myself,” she said. “I needed to be connected with that rage that I used to find solace in. I needed to be reminded that my voice, whether soft or loud, holds weight and creates tension that I get to be in control of. I can play a girl who has an internal world—crazy!—whom is the lead of a film and whom is not the perfect victim. Nikki was my opportunity to show the world what I knew I could do and to that, I have to say thank you.”

She went on to thank Obsession’s writer and director Curry Barker, along with all actresses who inspired her early on (including Bryce Dallas Howard, who presented Navarrette with the award). “Thank you not only to Curry Barker for writing a role that allowed me that chance, but thank you to all of the women that took that chance before me,” she said. “Thank you to Jessica Lange for showing me and all of her work that there is beauty in madness and to just give it to the world. Thank you to Natalie Portman for Léon: The Professional. It was the first time I got to see a kid in a movie being more than a kid. She was me. Thank you to Sally Field and Steel Magnolias for daring to portray grief honestly and the hardships of motherhood gracefully. Thank you, Bryce Dallas Howard and The Help, for portraying how intersectional womanhood can divide us and that women who look different than ourselves are no different than ourselves. Thank you to Florence Pugh, Carrie Coon, Julianne Moore, Elizabeth Debicki, Samantha Morton, Rosalind Eleazar, Penélope Cruz. Thank you for daring to be wild. Thank you. Incredibly, you have all given me permission to be free. Let’s all shine a light in each other.”

Xbox Fans Urge Caution Over New Zealand Trick For GTA 6, While Others Say It's A Non-Issue

📅 Nov 17, 2026  |  Category: Entertainment  |  Read Source →

"They literally told me this in person in Seattle at Xbox FanFest last week"

We've already talked a little bit about the New Zealand trick and how it's pretty much the number one topic on social media when it comes to Xbox and GTA 6 right now, so it's naive to think nobody's going to try it this Wednesday.

If you're not in the loop, this refers to people changing their region on Xbox to access GTA 6 at local midnight time in New Zealand, which equates to 6am EST / 11am GMT on Wednesday, November 18th.

News Here's What Xbox Owners Need To Know About 'Moving To New Zealand' For GTA 6

Over the past couple of days, there's been a ton of debate online about whether it's "OK" or "safe" to switch regions in order to play GTA 6 early on Xbox Series X and Xbox Series S, and if you listen to what the majority say, the answer is that Xbox "don't care" if you do it, and that it's happened on many occasions without bans in the past.

However, it's true that Xbox specifically states in its TOCs that you shouldn't change regions unless you've actually moved to the country in question, and over on X/Twitter today, Windows Central editor Jez Corden and YouTuber Colt Eastwood revealed that they talked about this exact topic during a recent visit to Xbox's headquarters in Seattle.

Here's what they had to say about it in response to another of WC's writers (apologies for the profanity in the tweet!):

A lot of people still believe that the risk is extremely low though, with some even arguing that it's "fear mongering" to suggest that Xbox will start suspending or banning people for wanting to play GTA 6 a few hours early.

There are others in the industry, such as The Verge editor Tom Warren, who also believe it's a non-issue:

The reality is that nobody knows for sure whether Xbox will take action against people switching their consoles to New Zealand to play GTA 6 early this Wednesday, but based on historical evidence, it seems pretty unlikely.

Is it worth taking the risk though? That's the bit we'll leave up to you, and speaking personally, I would never want to recommend doing something like this in case Microsoft suddenly decides to throw the ban hammer around.

Let's wait and see how it all pans out on Wednesday, eh?

Jennifer Love Hewitt Says Marriage With Brian Hallisay Is a “Choice”

📅 Nov 17, 2026  |  Category: Entertainment  |  Read Source →

Jennifer Love Hewitt detailed why she sees her 13-year marriage to husband Brian Hallisay as an everyday “choice,” noting she feels “very fortunate” to have him as a partner.

Jennifer Love Hewitt is a fan of putting her middle name into practice.

More than a decade into her marriage with Brian Hallisay, the 9-1-1 actress shared why her “heart still flutters” when she thinks about her husband.

“For us, it’s a choice every day,” Hewitt told People in an interview published Nov. 16. “That’s the romantic part. I don’t ever expect him to choose me. I want to earn that choice every day.”

But while the 47-year-old admitted her romance with Hallisay “ebbs and flows,” she is always reminded of how much she loves him when they’re apart for too long.

“I still want him to be right there. And it’s a good feeling,” she continued. “I think we just feel lucky and we keep it going.”

Since meeting on set in 2011 and tying the knot in 2013, Hewitt and Hallisay have welcomed three kids: Autumn, 12, Atticus, 11, and Aidan, 5. And having her husband on her team is a heartwarming change of pace.

“I didn’t have that growing up myself, and so I feel very fortunate that I have it,” she added. “He’s a really good partner. He’s a great dad. And he’s just a good man. And so I feel very lucky to have him.”

While Hewitt may know what you did last summer, she’s also been adamant that the secret to her marriage is opting to be dedicated to one another.

“We, I think, struggle like most parents with three kids and full-time jobs and all of the things to find our time,” she told People in 2024, “but I really do wake up and choose Brian every day, and I would choose him every time.”

As Hewitt sees it, “I genuinely feel that way, even when he makes me crazy. And he continues to choose me, thank God.”

NCIS: New York Makes A Massive Breakthrough As Series Reaches Major Milestone

📅 Nov 17, 2026  |  Category: Entertainment  |  Read Source →

The NCIS universe is showing no signs of slowing down anytime soon.

The NCIS franchise continues to expand its global presence, with its newest spinoff, NCIS: New York, hitting a major milestone after its debut. According to an exclusive report from Variety, the crime drama, starring LL Cool J, has secured distribution agreements in more than 135 international markets. The deals range from traditional TV networks to streaming services across six continents.

The show's international agreements include several major entertainment companies. Paramount+ has acquired the series for multiple territories, including Australia, Austria, Brazil, Germany, Ireland, Switzerland, the United Kingdom, and Latin America. Other confirmed distribution partners include Global and STACKTV (Canada), M-Net (Africa), Sky (New Zealand), and Amazon (Southeast Asia).

Audiences in Japan will have access through J:COM and WOWOW, while AXN has acquired the series for Latin America. Additionally, Disney (the Balkans), STAR Channel (Spain), Viaplay (Sweden, Denmark, and Norway), and Coupang Play (South Korea) also have deals with the series. The show has also been acquired by platforms in Belgium, Greece, Iceland, Israel, and the Netherlands.

The news comes after NCIS: New York premiered on CBS on October 6, 2026. The series brings Cool J back to the franchise as Sam Hanna, the character he portrayed for 14 seasons on NCIS: Los Angeles. That latter ended its run in May 2023. However, Hanna's story is not over as the new spinoff places him in a brand-new city with a different team of agents.

The newest chapter follows Hanna as he returns to New York City, where he grew up, for an assignment. At first, it seems like a pretty straightforward mission that was supposed to be temporary. Eventually, he realizes that there is far more than first meets the eye, forcing him to play a bigger role.

Hanna joins forces with Special Agent Nick Schaeffer, who is portrayed by Scott Caan. Together, the two characters help lead the team that is responsible for investigating cases involving national security and military personnel. NCIS: New York also stars Jennifer Beals (Robyn Wells), Jacqueline Byers (Addison Ross), Shane Harper (Wyatt Sellers), and Devin Druid (Sean Sullivan).

NCIS: New York airs new episodes on Tuesdays at 9:00 PM ET and 6:00 PM PT on CBS. The series will be taking a break during the week of Thanksgiving.

‘Percy Jackson And The Olympians’ Season 3 Release Schedule: When Do New Episodes Come Out?

📅 Nov 17, 2026  |  Category: Entertainment  |  Read Source →

A new tidal wave of Percy Jackson and the Olympians episodes will soon break on Disney+.

The third season of the television series adaptation based on Rick Riordan’s book series arrives the same day as The Hunger Games: Sunrise on the Reaping hits theaters. Deriving from the third book in the series, specifically, Season 3 charts new territory because Riordan’s The Titan’s Curse has never been adapted for the screen before.

The cast have teased a much darker season, and plenty of new faces will join as a new quest emerges for Percy (Walker Schobell) and Thalia (Tamara Smart). Read on for all the details about the Percy Jackson and the Olympians Season 3 release schedule:

When does Season 3 of Percy Jackson and the Olympians come out? Watch on Deadline

Percy Jackson and the Olympians Season 3 premieres Friday, November 20.

How many episodes are in Season 3 of Percy Jackson and the Olympians? Will they release weekly or all at once?

Percy Jackson and the Olympians Season 3 will unfold with two episodes arriving each week for a total of four weeks. The first two episodes arrive at premiere on Nov. 20, and the finale and penultimate episode will arrive on Friday, December 11.

Who is in the cast of Percy Jackson and the Olympians Season 3?

Walker Scobell portrays Percy, son of Poseidon. Tamara Smart plays Thalia Grace, daughter of Zeus. Aryan Simhadri portrays Grover, the satyr and protector of Percy, Thalia and Annabeth. Leah Sava Jeffries portrays Annabeth Chase, daughter of Athena. Charlie Busnhell plays Luke Castellan, son of Hermes and turncoat. Glynn Turman portrays Chiron, centaur and son of Kronos. Virginia Kull portrays Sally Jackson, Percy’s mother. Dior Goodjohn plays Clarisse La Rue. Jason Mantzoukas appears as Dionysus, the god of wine.

Daniel Diemer portrayed Percy’s half brother Tyson, a cyclops, in Season 2. Timothy Simons also appeared in Season 2 as Tantalus. Beatrice Kitsos portrayed Alison Simms. Adam Copeland appears as Ares.

Season 3 will also welcome Olive Abercrombie and Levi Chrisopulos as Bianca and Nico di Angelo, children of Hades. Dafne Keen will portray Artemis, goddess of the hunt, the moon and maidens. Saara Chadry, (The Mysterious Benedict Society, PAW Patrol: The Movie) will play Zoë Nightshade, Artemis’ lieutenant and an immortal hunter.

Kate McKinnon will appear as Aphrodite, goddess of love and beauty, who can change looks based on who she is encountering. Holt McCallany will portray the Titan Atlas, also known as The General of Kronos’ army. David Costabile will play Dr. Thorn, who readers meet at the very beginning of The Titan’s Curse. Jesse L. Martin will portray Annabeth’s mortal father, Frederick Chase.

Ming-Na Wen will play Hera, and Jennifer Beals will portray Demeter. Hubert Smielecki will play Artemis’ twin brother Apollo, god of the sun, archery and poetry as well as prophecy.

What is Percy Jackson and the Olympians Season 3 about?

The titular Titan’s curse refers to the burden Atlas usually bears by holding up the weight of the world or sky on his shoulders. Season 3 of the show will see Annabeth be kidnapped by Dr. Thorn, who is a manticore, as part of Atlas’ and Kronos’ plans.

Thus, Percy and Thalia must set aside their differences and join forces with the Hunters of Artemis to embark on a quest to save her and try once more to put a stop to Luke resurrecting the Titan Lord Kronos, who sired all of the gods and has tried to put an end to their reign before.

Roughly 90 minutes after the news broke that Pattinson was to lead Amazon MGM Studios reboot of the hit franchise, the royals joined hundreds of thousands of commentators on social media — but did so in a manner that really only they could.

“Welcome to His Majesty’s Secret Service, Mr Bond,” they posted on the royal family‘s official X account, with a link to the announcement. Related Stories Netflix Orders YA Drama 'Prep' Based on Curtis Sittenfeld Novel From Nicole Taylor 'Stranger Things' Prequel Spinoff 'Other Worlds' Ordered to Series at Netflix

As it happens, Pattinson will be the very first Bond to work for His Majesty’s Secret Service rather than Her Majesty’s Secret Service. Queen Elizabeth II was monarch across the entire slate of 25 titles until her passing in 2022, a year after the release of “No Time to Die,” the last 007 movie and Daniel Craig’s final stint as the super spy.

Welcome to His Majesty's Secret Service, Mr Bond. https://t.co/inKwKqHEDd

Pattinson’s casting brought to a close years of speculation over who incoming Bond director Denis Villenueve would hand the famed Walther PKK, speculation that turned into a frenzy over the last month as the names were whittled down. “James Bond is one of the most legendary characters in cinema, and to be entrusted with 007 is the honor of a lifetime,” Pattinson said in a statement. “I’m deeply grateful to Denis, Amy, David, Tanya and everyone at Amazon MGM Studios for their belief in me. 📅 Nov 16, 2026  |  Category: Entertainment  |  Read Source →

NEW DELHI: Suryakumar Yadav is making his intentions clear with the bat. The T20 World Cup-winning captain continued his stunning run in the Syed Mushtaq Ali Trophy, smashing a blistering 29-ball 82 to power Mumbai to a commanding five-wicket victory over Gujarat on Monday.It was his second consecutive fifty-plus score in the domestic T20 tournament, following his explosive 21-ball 60 against Hyderabad in Mumbai's opening match on November 14. The 36-year-old's latest assault featured nine fours and seven sixes as Mumbai chased down Gujarat's 198-run target in just 15.4 overs.Sarfaraz Khan provided the finishing support with an unbeaten 40 off 17 balls, ensuring Mumbai completed a dominant chase. Earlier, Shardul Thakur and Atharva Ankolekar had claimed three wickets apiece to restrict Gujarat, although Ashutosh Sharma (42 off 22) and Vishal Jayswal (38 off 21) lifted them to 197 with a late flourish.For Suryakumar, however, the runs carry significance beyond Mumbai's campaign. Having lost his place in the Indian side after leading the country to a second consecutive T20 World Cup title, the batter is determined to earn his way back.

After his innings against Hyderabad in the SMAT opener, Suryakumar spoke candidly about the disappointment of being left out of the Indian squad and his renewed motivation to represent the country."After winning the World Cup, you naturally expect that you will play after the IPL or whenever India's first series takes place. Because you have won a major tournament, and winning an ICC event is not easy, especially on home soil, something nobody had achieved before," he said."But the selectors must have had their reasons and must have thought things through before taking that decision. But yes, I was hurt. If you don't feel sad or hurt, how will you turn that into passion, and how will you play with that passion? That is what I am trying to do now," he added.The former India T20I captain said the hunger to play for the country had returned and that he was ready to work his way back into the side."The same hunger I had earlier to play for India, when I used to score runs after returning to domestic cricket, has come back again. If I want to play for India again, I will have to break the door down once more. All right, let's do it," he said.His appointment as Mumbai captain has also given him an opportunity to lead from the front. Suryakumar expressed gratitude to Mumbai Cricket Association president Ajinkya Naik for backing him during a difficult phase and entrusting him with the responsibility."He could have chosen someone else. He had that option, but the responsibility, love and respect he has given me mean a lot. I will always remain grateful and humble for that," he said.

Suryakumar also spoke about adapting to the changing demands of T20 cricket, with teams increasingly targeting totals of 250 and beyond. He stressed the need to sustain an attacking approach through the middle overs rather than allowing the scoring rate to dip after the powerplay."After that, when we bat between overs seven and 15, it is important to maintain the same intensity and intent, even when playing for India. If we have to score 250-260, then that is what we have to do, and I am trying to follow that approach," he said."The 'high-risk, high-reward' game in the middle overs has always been part of my batting. But now I am trying to understand how I can take even more risks and hit more boundaries and sixes because T20 cricket has become a format dominated by boundaries and sixes," he added.Asked whether his innings against Hyderabad was intended as a message to the selectors, Suryakumar insisted that his focus was on enjoying his batting, scoring consistently and helping Mumbai win."It is not about making a statement. I have been batting this way for the last four or five years, and I am enjoying it just as much today. This is an entertainment format, so I want to go out there and express myself freely," he had said.

52 Years Later, One Of Sci-Fi's Most Legendary Space Operas Finally Gets Its Streaming Finale

📅 Nov 16, 2026  |  Category: Entertainment  |  Read Source →

One of pop culture's longest-running space operas celebrates the end of an era. The space opera genre is most famously represented by Star Wars, the movie-turned-franchise that overhauled sci-fi cinema with its revolutionary use of VFX and SFX before creating a massive multimedia empire that only continues to grow half a century later. Dune became a phenomenon of its own earlier than Star Wars, though it only met its full potential thirty-seven years after its first movie adaptation and fifty-six years after the first novel's publication. On television, Star Trek pioneered optimistic cosmic exploration and Battlestar Galactica brought survival-driven drama to extraterrestrial fleet warfare.

Tucked beneath those household names, dozens of underrated space operas still await the mainstream recognition they deserve. The Jim Henson Company's Farscape is a unique title in the genre due to its high-quality puppetry, and Babylon 5 popularized serialized five-year television storytelling long before the streaming era. In animation, Titan A.E., Crest of the Stars, and Outlaw Star delivered heartfelt starfaring adventures that often get overshadowed by bigger franchises. There are also countless literary gems like Vernor Vinge’s A Fire Upon the Deep, Lois McMaster Bujold’s Vorkosigan Saga, and Arkady Martine’s A Memory Called Empire.

Right alongside Star Wars and Star Trek, a fan-favorite space opera reaches a new milestone half a century after its first installment.

Fifty-two years after the original 1974 series, one of anime's most influential space operas has delivered its complete streaming finale with all twenty-six episodes of Star Blazers: Space Battleship Yamato 3199. The series' final chapter weaves together the 1980 theatrical feature Be Forever Yamato and the third classic television season, Space Battleship Yamato III, into a cohesive new timeline. Set in the year 2207, Star Blazers: Space Battleship Yamato 3199 follows Susumu Kodai and the scattered veterans of the Yamato as they reunite in secret after a colossal structure called the Grand Reverse breaches Earth's defenses and allows the enigmatic Dezarium army to occupy the capital.

Star Blazers: Space Battleship Yamato 3199 presents a striking blend of classic mechanical designs and mature military drama that respects decades of canon and keeps veteran fans guessing at every turn, plus sweeping orchestral music that makes the space opera feel even more majestic. The seventh and final chapter adds episodes 23 through 26 to the catalog after its October 2026 theatrical debut in Japan. With the entire twenty-six-episode arc all available at once, newcomers and longtime admirers alike can experience the complete saga without interruption after more than fourteen years of anticipation.

Star Blazers: Space Battleship Yamato 3199 brings classic space opera back to its roots with every classic space opera trope that originally inspired modern sci-fi icons like Star Wars and Battlestar Galactica. Throughout its seven chapters, 3199 delivers daring espionage behind enemy lines and thundering fleet battles led by captain Susumu Kodai, tactical ace Yuki Mori, veteran tactician Shiro Sanada, and the rapidly growing Sasha alongside the menacing Dezarium commander Alphon. Standout installments like the tense opening invasion in Chapter 1, the underground resistance intrigue of Chapter 3, and the breathtaking battles in Chapters 6 and 7 showcase breathtaking choreography and Akira Miyagawa's lush orchestral score.

If you love Star Trek, Captain Kirk’s ongoing series needs to be on your radar, especially if you’re a lore fanatic.

Now that all twenty-six episodes of 3199 have wrapped up the series, the future of the Space Battleship Yamato franchise looks remarkably bright as it enters its second half-century. Since launching the modern remake continuity with Yamato 2199 in 2012, studios like Xebec, Satelight, and Studio Mother have reimagined the classic 1970s and early-1980s saga to immense critical and commercial success. Given the warm reception to 3199 and chief director Harutoshi Fukui's clear passion for the material, a new installment could present a modern reimagining of the 1983 theatrical epic Final Yamato and its legendary Planet Aquarius crisis alongside the upcoming movie Cosmoship Yamato √2199.

Coming into the year, there was plenty of anticipation for Ridley Scott’s return to the sci-fi genre with his new movie, The Dog Stars. Scott even enlisted some big-time stars such as Josh Brolin and Jacob Elordi for roles in the film, but it led to nothing more than a disappointing run at the box office.

Scott has a few exciting projects on the horizon, though, not least of which being his new Treasure Island reboot with Hugh Jackman. Last year, Scott took his producing talents to Apple TV to work on the electric psychological thriller, Echo Valley, which stars Sydney Sweeney opposite Julianne Moore. The movie premiered on Apple TV last year in June, but it overstayed its welcome on watchlists, where it’s now been in the top 10 this year for over 275 days. It joins the ranks of other Apple TV smash hits like The Gorge and Greyhound that have managed to hit this colossal milestone this year.

Four criminal masterminds. One of them was always going to be yours. Here is who comes for you, why they picked you out of everyone available, and what they want from the encounter.

Your nemesis does not want to beat you. He wants to know you completely, and he is patient enough to wait for it. The frightening part is how good the conversation is.

Your nemesis is not after money, territory or revenge. He is after a demonstration, and you are the person he has chosen to prove his point on.

Your nemesis respects you, which sounds flattering until you work out what it means. He chose you because nobody else was worth his time.

Your nemesis has no personal interest in you whatsoever, and that is precisely what makes him unsurvivable. You are not a rival. You are a stop on the way.

“In the edge-of-your-seat thriller Echo Valley, Kate (Julianne Moore) is a mother struggling to make peace with her troubled daughter Claire (Sydney Sweeney), a situation that becomes even more perilous when Claire shows up on Kate's doorstep, hysterical and covered in someone else's blood. As Kate pieces together the shocking truth of what happened, she learns just how far a mother will go to try to save her child in this gripping tale of love, sacrifice and survival from BAFTA-winning director Michael Pearce and Emmy-nominated writer Brad Ingelsby.”

‘The Odyssey’ Cinematographer Hoyte Van Hoytema Breaks Down Filming Climactic Thunderstorm Scene in Imax

📅 Nov 16, 2026  |  Category: Entertainment  |  Read Source →

Christopher Nolan shot “The Odyssey” to be experienced on the biggest screen possible so audiences could feel the full impact and be fully immersed in that experience — so everything was shot practically.

One of the most challenging sequences to pull off was the film’s final storm scene as Matt Damon’s Odysseus faces the wrath of Poseidon and the full force of Zeus. It was to be climactic and intense, and Odysseus loses all his men as a result of the final insult to the gods. Related Stories 'Nobody Wants This' Season 3 Opens With 6.2 Million Views in Four Days Netflix U.K. Layoffs Hit Up to 3% of London HQ, Production VP Anna Mallett Exits

“We had been talking about going out there to sea, sit on real boats and shoot on Imax,” cinematographer Hoyte Van Hoytema explains.

A large amount of the film was shot on the open ocean, where Nolan, his cast and crew spent four months capturing how hard the journey would have been as they endured crashing waves. And while the climactic moment ended up being shot in Los Angeles, Nolan didn’t spare any expense in pulling off the sequence, relying instead on old-fashioned filmmaking.Speaking with Variety for Inside the Frame, Van Hoytema explains the scene provided a challenge: “How to replicate something and make it feel visceral and real? How can we do that and avoid a huge amount of CG?”Two decommissioned jet engines were used as wind machines. Van Hoytema’s chief lighting technician and gaffer Adam Chambers built light boxes that would emulate thunder and lightning. The boat was then put on a gimbal, all while being shot on an Imax camera. “It was like shooting a real storm, just under safety supervision,” Van Hoytema says.He adds, “We did a lot of different setups in that whole scene. It is designed to feel extremely chaotic; it’s designed to sort of not understand really what’s going on.”The lighting design was something he also had to think about. “It was interesting because I was thinking, if you’re out at sea at night 2,000 years ago, without electricity and you end up in a thunderstorm, what would that feel like? I was thinking, if you can see everything around you, that’s going to look fake.”

A CGI storm didn’t feel right because the entire crew had been meticulous about keeping everything as grounded and as experiential as possible. Van Hoytema says the team built overhead lighting rigs that were approximately 60×30 feet, hanging on cranes above the ship. “It meant that you would see flashes that progressively move from behind them to the front of them, as it opens up the sky. “What you see is short bursts and there’s just darkness or a little bit of a glow. It’s like trying to see this relentless endless sea, this weather, this saltwater and they’re just so close to death. It’s extremely claustrophobic at the same time.”

When it came to framing Odysseus, Van Hoytema was wired in to keep him in place on the gimbal. His Imax camera on his shoulder was also wired in.

“I was not trying to be in any way precious about framing whatsoever. I was just trying through with a jet engine blowing, gallons of water in my face. I was literally just trying to identify if it was even Matt Damon in the shot. It was extremely visceral, and it’s almost hard to grasp. It was not a situation in which you could just start a take or not. Every take was an event.”

Van Hoytema says the camera starts to roll and the jet engines start. “They rise, and at some point, they sound like two fighter jets are next to your ear. And it gets really wild because they start moving the gimbal as well, and you’re holding on for your life. The camera gets turned; it’s the original Imax camera, not the blimp and the Imax camera is very loud. However, in that situation, you don’t even know if it’s turned on. You do a take, and you have a crazy powerful adrenaline rush.”

In contrast, Van Hoytema recalled a moment when the crew was out to sea, in open waters and a pod of bottlenose dolphins appeared. It was completely unplanned.

Van Hoytema recalls, “It’s interesting because it was also pretty violent that day. It was very harsh weather and I remember a lot of people on the crew got seasick. As we started filming, dolphins started to jump out of the water. What is interesting is that it symbolizes that no matter what the weather does, we take the weather as it comes and accept it as the reality you work in that day.”

→ Baca artikel asli

Shondaland Taps Amy Joy Rivkin and Karyn Smith-Forge for Key Executive Roles

📅 Nov 16, 2026  |  Category: Entertainment  |  Read Source →

Rivkin will be vp production at the company, and Smith-Forge will serve as vp scripted series.

Shondaland is adding to its executive ranks.

The production company founded by Shonda Rhimes and Betsy Beers has named Amy Joy Rivkin as vp production and Karyn Smith-Forge as vp scripted series. Rivkin comes to Shondaland from Media Res, and Smith-Forge from Don Cheadle’s production company This Radicle Act.

“Amy Joy’s impressive track record steering complex, premium productions and Karyn’s vast experience and sharp instincts across scripted genres make them powerful additions to Shondaland,” said Alison Eakle, Shondaland’s chief content officer. “We’re thrilled we get to shape our next chapter of storytelling alongside them.” Related Stories TV Shonda Rhimes Signs New Overall Deal at Netflix TV Kim Raver on Leaving 'Grey's Anatomy': "Who Gets to Play a Character for This Long?"

Rivkin will oversee physical production on Shondaland’s shows, which include Netflix’s Bridgerton, ABC’s Grey’s Anatomy and its upcoming spinoff and a number of projects in development. She spent the past four years at The Morning Show and Pachinko producer Media Res. Prior to that, she was at Netflix, handling production management for the streamer’s original drama series, and Legendary Entertainment.

As vp scripted series, Smith-Forge will lead development of new series from Shondaland at Netflix, where the company has a rich overall deal. Smith-Forge was most recently president of This Radicle Act, working with Cheadle on TV and film projects at a number of outlets, including Netflix, Showtime, AMC, FX and HBO Max.

She was previously senior vp production at Berlanti Productions, where she managed a slate of some 20 series, including The CW’s Arrow-verse.

James Bond Fans React To Robert Pattinson's 007 Casting

📅 Nov 16, 2026  |  Category: Entertainment  |  Read Source →

Robert Pattinson has spent his career ping-ponging between major franchise fare ("Harry Potter," "Twilight," "The Batman") and weird arthouse fare ("The Lighthouse," "Good Time," "Cosmopolis"). Along the way, he's garnered a pretty broad fanbase, so there are plenty of people celebrating the news that Pattinson will be taking over the role of James Bond.

There were several other finalists to play the next Bond, including Josh O'Connor, Jack Lowden, and Nicholas Hoult, but Pattinson was the clear frontrunner even before he was announced. Early reactions to the news have noted the somewhat absurd flex of landing the role of James Bond just a few years after landing the role of Batman. "This is the greed they talk about in the Bible," wrote one user on Reddit.

Casting such a well-known actor in the role of Bond bucks the trend of 007 casting, with previous revamps traditionally favoring actors who weren't already firmly established. Many people, while not necessarily disliking the choice of Pattinson for the role, have pointed out that Amazon isn't exactly taking a risk. Other Reddit users describe Pattinson as "a very conservative choice" and "very safe casting," with one wryly commenting, "This isn't beating the allegations of 'every movie has the same five actors in it.'" So, while casting a lesser-known actor would surely have attracted some controversy in its own right, some Bond fans feel that the safety of this choice is "disappointing."

And, of course, there are Batman fans who fear that this fresh commitment spells the end of Pattinson's tenure as the Dark Knight. Between production delays on "The Batman II," a recent power shake-up at DC Studios, and now this 007 casting news, the general assumption is that Pattinson's Batman will be hanging up his cape after two movies.

One of the upsides of having such an eccentric career and personality is that Robert Pattinson has generated a great many memes over the years — and now they're being put to good use. One Tumblr user adapted a famous "Twilight" scene to have Pattinson's Edward Cullen saying, "This is the skin of a [license to kill]er," Bella." Others, meanwhile, have joked that we "finally have a Gen Z James Bond," in reference to a Vanity Fair interview wherein Pattinson doggedly insists that he is Gen Z and that the year he was born "doesn't matter."

One meme that became popular even before Pattinson was officially cast imagines the reactions of the other hopefuls auditioning for the role when they see the unstoppable Pattinson walk in after them.

every other actor when robert pattinson shows up to audition pic.twitter.com/40fHsnS2C4

— ivy (@ohhhhhherewegoo) November 16, 2026

Fans are particularly amused by the fact that Pattinson showed up "late" to the audition process (which has been going on for years), sandwiching it in between his commitments to "Dune: Part Three" and "The Batman Part II," and still landed the role. But that kind of effortless swagger only makes him more perfect for the role of James Bond.

Vikings won't be leaving Minnesota for altitude acclimation

📅 Nov 16, 2026  |  Category: Entertainment  |  Read Source →

With quarterback Kirk Cousins back and the Minnesota defense playing at a championship level, the sky is the limit for the Vikings.

On Sunday, the extent to which they’ll be literally in the sky could limit the ability to get an eighth win.

The Week 11 prime-time showdown between the 7-2 Vikings and 8-1 49ers will happen in Mexico City, where the altitude is 7,280 feet above sea level. The Vikings won’t be relocating this week to acclimate to the thinner air.

The 49ers, in contrast, have gone from Dallas to Colorado, where they’ll train in the far lower distance of 5,280 feet above sea level.

The Vikings will have a plan to get their players ready to perform on an elevated field. Details will emerge this week.

Whatever the plan is, it won’t entail the same kind of direct acclimation in which the 49ers are engaging.

It sets up another apples-to-apples comparison regarding the plans for international games. In Week 1, the 49ers went to Australia early. The Rams went late. And the 49ers dominated L.A., winning 27-7.

This time around, the stakes are extremely high. The winner takes over the de facto driver’s seat for the top seed in the NFC.

A loss means more to the Vikings than the 49ers, as it relates to their respective divisions. San Francisco has a solid cushion over the Seahawks and Rams in the NFC West. The Vikings are caught in a three-way tie for first place in the NFC North.

Dave Franco & Willa Fitzgerald To Lead Lionsgate Horror Film ‘Mommy’s Home’

📅 Nov 16, 2026  |  Category: Entertainment  |  Read Source →

EXCLUSIVE: Over the summer, Universal unleased one menacing mommy upon theaters — and now, Lionsgate is preparing to unleash another. Dave Franco and Willa Fitzgerald have signed on to star in Mommy’s Home, the studio’s new psychological horror film to be directed by David F. Sandberg.

Details as to the duo’s roles are being kept under wraps. Sandberg, the director of Annabelle: Creation and Lights Out, helms the story of a young dad whose family life becomes terrifyingly unhinged when his cryogenically preserved mom is unfrozen and sent to live with him and his family. Related Stories Casting Aaron Pierre And 'Sisu' Director Jalmari Helander Team on Action Thriller 'Fairytale In New York' From Netflix And Lionsgate Acquisitions Lionsgate Acquires Alma Har'el's 'Wet' Starring Connor Storrie, Rebecca Ferguson, Javier Bardem, Odessa A'zion, Rosalía & Lukas Haas

The project marks a reteam for Lionsgate and Franco following his starring role in the studio’s hit threequel Now You See Me: Now You Don’t, which grossed over $245 million worldwide last fall. Franco and Fitzgerald reteam after co-starring in Paramount’s 2025 Colleen Hoover adaptation Regretting You. Watch on Deadline

The script for Mommy’s Home is by James Morosini, who was attached to direct when we announced the project back in 2024. Producers include Craig Flores, Dawn Olmstead and Anonymous Content, along with Sandberg and Lotta Losten via Mångata. Nathan Kahane and Morosini will exec produce, as will Franco, joined by David Levine and Garrett Kemble on behalf of Anonymous Content.

Scott O’Brien and Maria Ascanio are overseeing the project for Lionsgate. Dan Freedman negotiated the deal on behalf of the studio, which launched international sales out of this year’s AFM.

Lionsgate Motion Picture Group President Erin Westerman told us, “Every once in a while, a script comes along that is so wildly original and impossible to forget that you just have to make it. Mommy’s Home is one of those movies. David F. Sandberg is an exceptional horror filmmaker, and we can’t imagine anyone better suited to bring this particular brand of madness to life.”

Westerman added, “Dave Franco is one of those rare actors who is endlessly watchable and impossible not to root for, even when the world around him is going completely off the rails. He’s been part of the Lionsgate family through Now You See Me, and we couldn’t be happier to be working with him again. And Willa Fitzgerald is going to be a revelation in this film. This is a project that everyone involved is making out of sheer passion, and we can’t wait for audiences to experience just how far it goes.”

Coming off the hard-R Sundance comedy Idiots and DreamWorks’ Forgotten Island, Franco will soon be seen reprising as himself in Season 2 of The Studio. This follows his Emmy nomination for a scene-stealing turn as himself in the CinemaCon-set Season 1 finale. He is repped by WME, Anonymous Content and Toczek Suddleson.

Breaking out with her turn in JT Mollner’s lauded psychological thriller Strange Darling, Fitzgerald has also been seen starring in Season 1 of the hit Amazon series Reacher, as well as Kathryn Bigelow’s Netflix film A House of Dynamite and Regretting You. Upcoming, she’ll be seen in the espionage thriller Billion Dollar Spy, as well as the Sony horror film Skeletons from Strange Darling‘s Mollner and producer J.J. Abrams. She is repped by CAA, Untitled and Jackoway Austen Tyerman.

Rihanna Addresses EJ Johnson Feud Rumors After Fashion Week Comment

📅 Nov 16, 2026  |  Category: Entertainment  |  Read Source →

Rihanna put rumors of a feud with EJ Johnson to bed over a month after the latter referenced her tardiness to a Dior show at Paris Fashion Week.

Rihanna always knows how to kiss it better.

After EJ Johnson raised eyebrows with a video detailing Rihanna’s tardiness to a Paris Fashion Week show—and when her apparent clap back raised eyebrows even higher—the “Umbrella” singer took a moment to confirm there’s no lingering bad blood.

“There’s no beef, you know there’s no beef,” she said in a video shared by TMZ. “I just felt bad for him, you know?”

In fact, she’s willing to put her best foot forward with EJ—or rather, allow them to.

When asked whether she’d ever let EJ walk in a Savage x Fenty show, the 38-year-old affirmed, “He deserves to.”

But while Rihanna proved it’s all loveeeeeee with EJ these days, there was a period when fans thought the pair had everything but love on the brain.

ICYMI, during Paris Fashion Week back in September, EJ shared a video recapping their experience at Dior’s Ready to Wear Spring/Summer 2027 collection.

“We just left the Dior show, getting out of there was crazy but the show itself was beautiful,” the Rich Kids of Beverly Hills alum said in the vlog. “It did start an hour late because Rihanna was late and, you know, like, we didn’t care to wait for her, but the show was beautiful nonetheless.”

Then, just a few days later, a video circulated on social media in which EJ could be seen posing for paparazzi in Paris, before making their way into their hotel. EJ’s way was soon blocked, however, by hotel staff, creating a kind of standstill witnessed by a crowd of fans and photographers.

And while Rihanna’s one-word response to the video (“Damn”) was originally interpreted as shade, garnering thousands of likes, her new comments reveal she just felt bad for the awkward moment EJ found themself in.

Besides, these days, Rihanna—mom to sons RZA, 4, Riot, 3, and daughter Rocki, 14 months, with A$AP Rocky—has been busy since she's been back in the studio, working on her first album in a decade.

It was A$AP who pulled back the proverbial curtain, revealing on The Jason Lee Show in August, “She in the studio right now.”

But even in the moments he said it, the rapper knew he’d just opened a massive can of worms.

“Yeah, I said it. Sorry, babe,” he joked. “Like, bro, she working. She cooking, no funny s--t.”

He then quipped, “Damn, I’ma get in trouble for this.”

As for EJ and Rihanna? While their rumored feud may have boiled down to nothing more than some misinterpreted comments, there are some Hollywood rivalries with much more heated waters. Read on for more.

The Sex and the City costars had dodged feud rumors for years, but they were reignited after Cattrall decided not to return for a third movie and made comments about her SLP that raised eyebrows.

"What was your reaction to Kim Cattrall telling Piers Morgan that you were never friends just colleagues?" Andy Cohen asked Parker on a September 2017 episode of Watch What Happens Live.

As she explained, "Just...heartbroken. I mean that whole week, you and I spoke about it because I was just...I don't know I found it really upsetting because that's, you know, that's not the way I recall our experience."

"So...it's sad but I'm kind of...I don't know, I always think that what ties us together is this singular experience is the thing," she continued. "It was a professional experience but it became personal because it was years and years of our lives. So I'm hoping that that sort of eclipses anything that's been recently spoken. That many years spent doing something so special that people had a connection with is such a privileged."

And though they didn't film scenes together, Cattrall did make a small appearance on And Just Like That.

During a 2022 TikTok challenge, JoJo Siwa described Candace Cameron Bure as the "rudest" celebrity, later explaining that she felt snubbed when Bure once refused to take a picture with her on a red carpet. Shortly after the dig, however, the Full House alum apologized: "I kind of broke your 11-year-old heart. I didn't take a picture with you. Ugh! I feel crummy, I feel—JoJo, I'm so sorry."

The two made amends. But months later, Cameron Bure was asked if she expects the Great American Family network, of which she serves as chief creative officer, to feature same-sex couples as leads in holiday movies. She said no, adding, "I think that Great American Family will keep traditional marriage at the core."

Siwa, who came out as a member of the LGBTQ+ community in 2021, wrote on Instagram that the actress' comment is "rude and hurtful to a whole community of people."

In August 2022, Morris and Pope traded harsh words with Aldean, wife of fellow country star Jason Aldean, after she shared an Instagram Reel of herself applying makeup, writing, "I'd really like to thank my parents for not changing my gender when I went through my tomboy phase. I love this girly life."

Berk (pictured second from left) addressed rumors of a falling out with France (middle) in January 2024, two months after he announced his exit from Queer Eye after eight seasons.

"I want people to know that Tan and I—we will be fine," he told Vanity Fair. "There was a situation, and that's between Tan and I, and it has nothing to do with the show. It was something personal that had been brewing—and nothing romantic, just to clarify that."

In March 2024, France responded to allegations that he had campaigned to replace Berk on Queer Eye with his friend Jeremiah Brent.

"Netflix and the production companies did a full-on casting. I didn't put my friend up for the job. They ended up getting it because they were the best person for the job," he said on Instagram. "But I didn't get them hired by getting rid of somebody else."

In September 2023, while playing a game called Stir the Pot with her daughter Kelly Osbourne on E! News, Sharon said the That '70s Show alum was the rudest celebrity she had ever met, branding him, a "rude, rude, rude, rude little boy" and a "dastardly little thing."

He did not respond to her remarks publicly.

Five years prior, Sharon told Larry King that when Ashton appeared on The Talk in 2014, he had an "attitude" after she got his name wrong.

"He goes, 'What are you, what have you done in this industry?'" she recalled. "And I was like, 'Kid, don't start with me, because I'm gonna eat you up and s--t you out.' So I was just like, 'You don't know what you're dealing with, kid.'"

Back in June 2021, the Hole frontwoman accused Rodrigo of copying her band's album cover for a promotional photo without giving credit, prompting the Disney alum to respond, "Love u and live through this sooooo much."

however, it seemed their hatchet was buried when Love replied, "Olivia - you're welcome. My favorite florist is in Notting Hill, London! Dm me for deets! I look forward to reading your note [winking face emoji]."

During a 2019 appearance on Watch What Happens Live, the Happy Days actor was asked why he didn't get along with the Forrest Gump star on the set of the 1989 flick Turner & Hooch.

"I was directing that movie for 13 days," Winkler recalled, "and then I was called into [producer] Jeff Katzenberg's office and he said, 'You have everything with you? Go home.'"

He did, however, seem to get along with Hanks' four-legged costar, quipping, "Love that dog."

Winkler later denied speculation that he still had beef with the Oscar winner, telling TMZ in 2020, "I don't have a feud with Tom Hanks."

Fans of The Hills will remember that iconic line, delivered by Conrad on the season three premiere. Their feud began when Montag started dating her now-husband Spencer Pratt, and hit its peak when Conrad accused her and Pratt of starting a rumor that she had made a sex tape with then-boyfriend Jason Wahler.

The costars reignited their ongoing feud in 2017 when Gibson publicly called out Johnson on Instagram after the Fast and Furious 9 release date was pushed back to April 2020. That same day, it was announced that a Fast and Furious spinoff starring Johnson and Jason Statham would hit theaters in 2019. The following month, Gibson threatened to quit Fast 9 if Johnson was in the movie.

Gibson then opened up about the feud, telling Van Lathan on The Red Pill podcast, "I have yet to talk to The Rock to this day, and we will have a conversation." He shared that he did have a chat with the former wrestler's producing partner, but not the actor himself.

"I found myself being the messenger on behalf of various people associated to the franchise, but stupid me was the only one who went public about those feelings, which is my own fault," gibson said. "It's not professional, it's not cool."

Swift and Perry had been in a feud for years, with their rift even allegedly inspiring the former's song "Bad Blood." The drama, which reportedly started over a backup dancer and competing tours, came to an end after Perry sent Swift a literal olive branch before her Reputation Tour kickoff in 2018.

"So I just got to my dressing room and found this actual olive branch," Swift said in an Instagram video. "This mean so much."

She captioned the video, "Thank you Katy."

The iconic singers have been feuding since 2004. While accepting the Classic Songwriter Award at the Q Awards that year, John said to attendees, "Madonna, best f--king live act? F--k off! Since when has lip-syncing been live? Sorry about that, but I think everyone who lip-syncs in public on stage, when you pay like 75 quid to see them, should be shot."

The former Grey's Anatomy star sparked a feud with the series creator in 2008 when she removed her name from the Emmy Awards consideration. Heigl stated that her reason for the move was because she wasn't "given the material to warrant a nomination."

Rhimes later opened up about Heigl's decision during an interview with Oprah Winfrey on Oprah's Next Chapter in 2012. "On some level it stung and on some level I was not surprised," she said. "When people show you who they are, believe them. I carry that [mantra] with me a lot. It has served me well."

It was the New York Fashion Week fight heard 'round the world when Nicki and Cardi got into a heated altercation at the Harper's Bazaar Icon party. The following month, Cardi opened up about the catalyst for the fight with Nicki.

"For a while now she's been taking a lot of shots at me," Cardi said in W magazine's Art Issue. "I spoke to her twice before, and we came to an understanding. But she kept it going."

The real spark of the fight came when Cardi saw that Nicki had allegedly liked and then unliked a tweet about Cardi's parenting skills, which Nicki has denied.

"I was going to make millions off my Bruno Mars tour, and I sacrificed that to stay with my daughter," Cardi continued. "I love my daughter. I'm a good-ass f--king mom. So for somebody that don't have a child to like that comment? So many people want to say that party wasn't the time or the place, but I'm not going to catch another artist in the grocery store or down the block."

Tension between Kanye and Taylor first started in 2009, when the "Stronger" rapper took the stage during her acceptance speech at the MTV Video Music Awards. Years later, the two would make up and even become friends, but that came to an end in 2016 after the release of his song "Famous," in which he name-checks the "End Game" singer.

In February 2025, one day before the Grammys, Kanye updated his Instagram to follow only Taylor. He added no further comment, and his fellow musician did not respond publicly to the move.

Carey and J.Lo's supposed feud started when the Queen of Christmas was asked to give her opinion on several singers during a 2003 German TV interview. When asked her opinion on Lopez, she replied, "I don't know her."

‘He Needs To Be Layered’: Bond Girl Léa Seydoux Shares the Most Important Traits for the Next 007

📅 Nov 16, 2026  |  Category: Entertainment  |  Read Source →

What it takes to play James Bond, from the actor who’s played his most influential love interest.

If there’s one Bond girl who has influenced the course of 007 history more than anyone, it’s Dr. Madeleine Swann. Played by French actor Léa Seydoux, who is this month’s ELLE UK cover star, psychotherapist Dr. Swann disrupts the James Bond narrative in a way that no previous lover has done: She challenges his misogyny.

Appearing in the last two films in the series—Spectre (2015) and No Time to Die (2021)—Dr. Swann has a long-term relationship with secret agent 007, eventually telling him that he’s the father of her daughter, making Seydoux’s part in the legendary franchise unique.

Asked whether she would consider a return to the franchise, now under the creative direction of Amazon MGM Studios and without Daniel Craig as 007, the actor said: “I really loved working with Barbara Broccoli and her brother Michael [G. Wilson]. It was one of the best experiences, a pure delight. And I don’t know, it will be different.”

She has already worked with Denis Villeneuve, the director attached to the next Bond film, in Dune: Part Two. “He’s a great choice, let’s see. I’m curious how they will reinvent it. But for me, it will never be the same because it was Barbara’s father who initiated the Bond movies. Now, I don’t know,” she told ELLE. “It became American….I hope they will keep the British spirit.”

As for casting Seydoux in the role, former Bond producer Barbara Broccoli said that her scenes in No Time to Die still move her to tears. “When we were filming the goodbye scene at the Italian train station, Léa approached it with such subtlety—she grasped her stomach to express her pain, but also the secret heartbreak that she was carrying James’s child. It moved me to tears then and continues to move me each time I watch it.”

As for the question of who will play Bond, she won’t be drawn into the speculation. (This was before Robert Pattinson was announced to take on the mantle on November 16.) Seydoux admits that finding the right person to replace Daniel Craig is difficult. “He needs to be layered, you know? And that is something that’s hard to find in young actors.”

Léa Seydoux appears next in Gentle Monster, which begins streaming on Netflix on November 18.

6 Superhero Movies From the 2000s That Are 10/10 Masterpieces

📅 Nov 16, 2026  |  Category: Entertainment  |  Read Source →

The 2000s were huge years for superhero cinema—arguably the biggest for the landscape of all time. It was in this decade that superhero movies, as audiences know them today, were defined, and the standards were set. The years 2000 to 2009 are some of the most significant years and helped birth some of the most popular franchises in cinematic history as a whole.

Sam Raimi's Spider-Man series, the X-Men franchise, the Marvel Cinematic Universe, Christopher Nolan's The Dark Knight series and so much more all got their big breaks in this era. Without these abundantly important 10 years, superhero movies would not even be close to where they are today. Their significance wouldn't even be close to what they are, and events like Avengers: Endgame most likely would not have ever happened or had the success they did without these foundational projects that are, to simply state, masterpieces.

Spider-Man is the most profitable superhero on the planet—he has been for quite some time now—and that meant that no one was surprised that, when the technology was finally there, he'd get a live-action movie. Come 2002, fans were finally treated to Sam Raimi's Spider-Man, which seriously rocked the world. Sure, X-Men came out just a few years earlier, but Spider-Man really helped drive home and fully prove that comic book characters could work on the big screen and the Hollywood landscape.

This 2002 flick was an all-time smash hit and proved that superhero movies could be genuinely amazing financial investments for big studios, with the project grossing an astounding about $825 million worldwide, with a phenomenal $114.8 million opening weekend. It was the first film to ever cross $100 million in its opening weekend and if that's not proof of how great this movie is, then what could be? The performances across the board from especially the likes of Willem Dafoe are wonderful, the directing from Raimi shows how unashamed he was in the source material, and the CGI work was revolutionary for the times.

The first Blade movie was a big moment for comic book movies, being one of the first ones to ever be rated-R and be a success. So, when it came time for the sequel, Blade II, the stakes were high. Thankfully, legendary director Guillermo del Toro and leading man Wesley Snipes, delivered a project that was a great success. It turned the franchise from what the franchise was in Blade to a high-speed, thrilling, gothic, and all-around fire flick.

Guillermo del Toro continues to be a masterclass of a director here with his emphasis on practical effects, making the more horror-based elements of the project sell better than they ever could in CGI. Snipes brings his everything to the role—as per usual—and keeps Blade feeling like a character that the audience genuinely wants to keep watching.

The first X-Men movie was incredibly important. It established these amazing characters and began to send a great message about the effects of discrimination and bigotry. Mutants became the talk of the town, and Hugh Jackman stepped into the boots of Wolverine for the very first time. However, it's highly agreed upon that the sequel, X2: X-Men United, is the superior film for a plethora of reasons.

This is, without a doubt, the Star Wars: Episode V — The Empire Strikes Back for the X-Men franchise. The stakes are raised significantly, and the themes and messaging are more prominent than ever before. It has one of the best opening scenes in a superhero movie—Nightcrawler (Alan Cumming) infiltrating the White House to attack the President (Cotter Smith), brings more classic X-Men characters to the forefront, has a great villain in William Stryker (Brian Cox), and so much more.

2008 was arguably the biggest year in superhero cinema for quite some time because of two major movies: The Dark Knight and none other than the beginning of the MCU itself, Iron Man. Brought to life by the amazing Jon Favreau in the director's chair and Robert Downey Jr. as the Armored Avenger, this 2008 project birthed one of the most popular and profitable cinematic franchises ever put to screen—one that still runs in massive success to this very day.

This served not only as a major comeback for Downey Jr.'s career, but was a huge step in CGI for filmmaking, as well. Iron Man looks perfect in pretty much every shot, and is still looked back on to have better-looking CGI than some modern Marvel films. The biggest standout, however, is the performance from Downey Jr. He solidifies himself at Tony Stark and continued to play him for over 10 years after the release of Iron Man, now even returning to the MCU again in 2026—albeit as a new character, Doctor Doom. This movie is the majority reason the superhero movie landscape has become what it is today.

Four people, four completely different ideas of what it means to have somebody in your corner. Here is who picked up, what they would ask of you, and what you would get back.

You want the friend who does not leave. Steve is the one who turns up at the door without being asked, who remembers what you said you were worried about, and who has never once made you audition for his attention.

You want the friend who is genuinely exhausting and completely worth it. Tony will forget your birthday, then build you something at four in the morning that you did not know you needed, and hand it over as though it were nothing.

You want the friend who replies in four seconds and apologises for it. Peter is earnest in a way that is almost unfashionable, and he will take on far more than he can carry rather than admit that he is struggling.

You want the friend who insults your shoes, fixes your phone and then quietly rearranges your entire life for the better. Shuri does not do soft reassurance, and that is the reason you would believe her when she says you are fine.

The other half of the two major super-films from 2008 is the unforgettable The Dark Knight by super-director Christopher Nolan. This project took a far more gritty and grounded take on the character of Batman (Christian Bale), and set a new style for superhero movies. Even the Spider-Man franchise took a note from this movie's book, with The Amazing Spider-Man also taking a grounded take—which everyone believes is because of the success of The Dark Knight.

Much like Iron Man, The Dark Knight's biggest highlight is within a masterful performance . . . that of Heath Ledger as the Joker. This performance has gone on to be one of the most impactful, defining, and memorable performance in cinematic history as a whole. The Dark Knight is genuinely one of the most impactful superhero movies ever made.

There's no question that when it comes to superhero films that are masterpieces, Spider-Man 2 must enter the conversation. Raimi's big sequel is genuinely known as one of the best movie sequels period. It takes everything that was good about Spider-Man, enhances those things, and adds to them as well. This feels like a borderline perfect superhero movie, and is still considered one to this day. It's so beloved and astounding that it's fair to say it won't be forgotten for quite some time.

With great acting from almost every cast member, iconic action (who could forget the train scene?), great visuals (both in cinematography and visual effects), a heartfelt-yet-heartbreaking screenplay, and a once-again mesmerizing score from Danny Elfman, Spider-Man 2 deserves all the praise it's gotten over the decades. What's so interesting and well-done about Spider-Man 2 is the fact that before anything, it's a Peter Parker (Tobey Maguire) movie—not a Spider-Man movie. The heart and soul of the story lies within Peter and his struggle between his two identities, and it isn't solely fueled by the external conflict—which is more of what Spider-Man did a few years prior. This allows the second entry in this franchise to feel more like a character study before anything else.

We want to hear from you. Share your perspective in the comments below, and please keep the conversation respectful.
